78:T987,<p><dfn variants="Environment Records">Environment Record</dfn> is a specification type used to define the association of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Identifier">Identifier</a></emu-nt>s to specific variables and functions, based upon the lexical nesting structure of ECMAScript code. Usually an Environment Record is associated with some specific syntactic structure of ECMAScript code such as a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionDeclaration">FunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-BlockStatement">BlockStatement</a></emu-nt>, or a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-Catch">Catch</a></emu-nt> clause of a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-TryStatement">TryStatement</a></emu-nt>. Each time such code is evaluated, a new Environment Record is created to record the identifier bindings that are created by that code.</p>
    <p>Every Environment Record has an <var class="field">[[OuterEnv]]</var> field, which is either <emu-val>null</emu-val> or a reference to an outer Environment Record. This is used to model the logical nesting of Environment Record values. The outer reference of an (inner) Environment Record is a reference to the Environment Record that logically surrounds the inner Environment Record. An outer Environment Record may, of course, have its own outer Environment Record. An Environment Record may serve as the outer environment for multiple inner Environment Records. For example, if a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionDeclaration">FunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt> contains two nested <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionDeclaration">FunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>s then the Environment Records of each of the nested functions will have as their outer Environment Record the Environment Record of the current evaluation of the surrounding function.</p>
    <p>Environment Records are purely specification mechanisms and need not correspond to any specific artefact of an ECMAScript implementation. It is impossible for an ECMAScript program to directly access or manipulate such values.</p>

    74:["$","$L76",null,{"metadata":{"title":"9 Executable Code and Execution Contexts","filePath":"content/executable-code-and-execution-contexts.mdx"},"bottomContent":"$undefined","sourceCode":"$undefined","children":[["$","div",null,{"id":"nextra-skip-nav"}],["$","main",null,{"data-pagefind-body":true,"children":["$","div",null,{"id":"spec-container","className":"ecma-spec","children":["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-executable-code-and-execution-contexts","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"$undefined","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"9"}]," Executable Code and Execution Contexts"],"$undefined"]}],["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-environment-records","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"91-environment-records","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"9.1"}]," Environment Records"],["$","$L77",null,{"id":"91-environment-records"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$78"}}],"$L79","$L7a"]}],"$L7b","$L7c","$L7d","$L7e","$L7f","$L80","$L81","$L82","$L83","$L84","$L85","$L86"]}]}]}]]}]
87:T5e58,<p><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Records</a></emu-xref> can be thought of as existing in a simple object-oriented hierarchy where <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> is an abstract class with three concrete subclasses: <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records">Declarative Environment Record</a></emu-xref>, <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-object-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-object-environment-records">Object Environment Record</a></emu-xref>, and <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-global-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-global-environment-records">Global Environment Record</a></emu-xref>. <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-function-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-function-environment-records">Function Environment Records</a></emu-xref> and <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-module-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-module-environment-records">Module Environment Records</a></emu-xref> are subclasses of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records">Declarative Environment Record</a></emu-xref>.</p>
      <ul>
        <li>
          <p><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> (abstract)</p>
          <ul>
            <li>
              <p>A <em><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records">Declarative Environment Record</a></emu-xref></em> is used to define the effect of ECMAScript language syntactic elements such as <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionDeclaration">FunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>s, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-VariableDeclaration">VariableDeclaration</a></emu-nt>s, and <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-Catch">Catch</a></emu-nt> clauses that directly associate identifier bindings with <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types">ECMAScript language values</a></emu-xref>.</p>
              <ul>
                <li>
                  <p>A <em><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-function-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-function-environment-records">Function Environment Record</a></emu-xref></em> corresponds to the invocation of an ECMAScript <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref>, and contains bindings for the top-level declarations within that function. It may establish a new <code>this</code> binding. It also captures the state necessary to support <code>super</code> method invocations.</p>
                </li>
                <li>
                  <p>A <em><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-module-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-module-environment-records">Module Environment Record</a></emu-xref></em> contains the bindings for the top-level declarations of a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt>. It also contains the bindings that are explicitly imported by the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt>. Its <var class="field">[[OuterEnv]]</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-global-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-global-environment-records">Global Environment Record</a></emu-xref>.</p>
                </li>
              </ul>
            </li>
            <li>
              <p>An <em><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-object-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-object-environment-records">Object Environment Record</a></emu-xref></em> is used to define the effect of ECMAScript elements such as <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-WithStatement">WithStatement</a></emu-nt> that associate identifier bindings with the properties of some object.</p>
            </li>
            <li>
              <p>A <em><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-global-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-global-environment-records">Global Environment Record</a></emu-xref></em> is used for <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt> global declarations. It does not have an outer environment; its <var class="field">[[OuterEnv]]</var> is <emu-val>null</emu-val>. It may be prepopulated with identifier bindings and it includes an associated <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">global object</a></emu-xref> whose properties provide some of the global environment's identifier bindings. As ECMAScript code is executed, additional properties may be added to the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">global object</a></emu-xref> and the initial properties may be modified.</p>
            </li>
          </ul>
        </li>
      </ul>

      <p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> abstract class includes the abstract specification methods defined in <emu-xref href="#table-abstract-methods-of-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#table-abstract-methods-of-environment-records">Table 14</a></emu-xref>. These abstract methods have distinct concrete algorithms for each of the concrete subclasses.</p>
      <emu-table id="table-abstract-methods-of-environment-records" type="abstract methods" of="Environment Records" oldids="table-15,table-17,table-19,table-20,table-additional-methods-of-function-environment-records,table-additional-methods-of-global-environment-records,table-additional-methods-of-module-environment-records" data-num="14">

          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>
                Method
              </th>
              <th>
                Purpose
              </th>
              <th>
                Definitions
              </th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tr id="abstract-hasbinding">
            <td>
              HasBinding (
                <var>name</var>: a String,
              ): either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> a Boolean or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>
            </td>
            <td>
              It determines if an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> has a binding for <var>name</var>.
            </td>
            <td>
              It has concrete definitions in the following types:
              <emu-concrete-method-dfns for="HasBinding"></emu-concrete-method-dfns>
            </td>
          </tr>
          <tr id="abstract-createmutablebinding">
            <td>
              CreateMutableBinding (
                <var>name</var>: a String,
                <var>deletable</var>: a Boolean,
              ): either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> <emu-const>unused</emu-const> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>
            </td>
            <td>
              It creates a new but uninitialized mutable binding in an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref>. <var>name</var> is the text of the bound name. If <var>deletable</var> is <emu-val>true</emu-val> the binding may be subsequently deleted.
            </td>
            <td>
              It has concrete definitions in the following types:
              <emu-concrete-method-dfns for="CreateMutableBinding"></emu-concrete-method-dfns>
            </td>
          </tr>
          <tr id="abstract-createimmutablebinding">
            <td>
              CreateImmutableBinding (
                <var>name</var>: a String,
                <var>strict</var>: a Boolean,
              ): either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> <emu-const>unused</emu-const> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>
            </td>
            <td>
              It creates a new but uninitialized immutable binding in an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref>. <var>name</var> is the text of the bound name. If <var>strict</var> is <emu-val>true</emu-val> then attempts to set it after it has been initialized will always throw an exception, regardless of the strict mode setting of operations that reference that binding.
            </td>
            <td>
              It has concrete definitions in the following types:
              <emu-concrete-method-dfns for="CreateImmutableBinding"></emu-concrete-method-dfns>
            </td>

            </td>
            <td>

If <var>strict</var> is <emu-val>true</emu-val> and the binding does not exist, this will throw a <emu-val>ReferenceError</emu-val> exception. If a binding for <var>name</var> exists, this removes the binding and returns <emu-val>true</emu-val>. If the binding exists but cannot be removed, this returns <emu-val>false</emu-val>. This returns <emu-val>true</emu-val> if it does and <emu-val>false</emu-val> if it does not. If it returns <emu-val>true</emu-val> it implies that the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-function-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-function-environment-records">Function Environment Record</a></emu-xref>, although the reverse implication does not hold.

            </td>

8d:Tb02,<p>The abstract operation GetIdentifierReference takes arguments <var>envRecord</var> (an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> or <emu-val>null</emu-val>), <var>name</var> (a String), and <var>strict</var> (a Boolean) and returns either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-reference-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-reference-record-specification-type">Reference Record</a></emu-xref> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>. 93:T12d4,<p>A <dfn id="privateenvironment-record" variants="PrivateEnvironment Records">PrivateEnvironment Record</dfn> is a specification mechanism used to track <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names">Private Names</a></emu-xref> based upon the lexical nesting structure of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassDeclaration">ClassDeclaration</a></emu-nt>s and <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassExpression">ClassExpression</a></emu-nt>s in ECMAScript code. They are similar to, but distinct from, <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Records</a></emu-xref>. Each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record">PrivateEnvironment Record</a></emu-xref> is associated with a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassDeclaration">ClassDeclaration</a></emu-nt> or <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassExpression">ClassExpression</a></emu-nt>. Each time such a class is evaluated, a new <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record">PrivateEnvironment Record</a></emu-xref> is created to record the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names">Private Names</a></emu-xref> declared by that class.</p>
    <p>Each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record">PrivateEnvironment Record</a></emu-xref> has the fields defined in <emu-xref href="#table-privateenvironment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#table-privateenvironment-records">Table 18</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <emu-table id="table-privateenvironment-records" caption="PrivateEnvironment Record Fields" data-num="18">
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>
              Field Name
            </th>
            <th>
              Value Type
            </th>
            <th>
              Meaning
            </th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tr>
          <td>
            <var class="field">[[OuterPrivateEnvironment]]</var>
          </td>
          <td>
            a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record">PrivateEnvironment Record</a></emu-xref> or <emu-val>null</emu-val>
          </td>
          <td>
            The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record">PrivateEnvironment Record</a></emu-xref> of the nearest containing class. <emu-val>null</emu-val> if the class with which this <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record">PrivateEnvironment Record</a></emu-xref> is associated is not contained in any other class.
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td>
            <var class="field">[[Names]]</var>
          </td>
          <td>
            a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names">Private Names</a></emu-xref>
          </td>
          <td>
            The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names">Private Names</a></emu-xref> declared by this class.
          </td>
        </tr>
      </table>
    </emu-table>

    7b:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-privateenvironment-records","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"92-privateenvironment-records","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"9.2"}]," PrivateEnvironment Records"],["$","$L77",null,{"id":"92-privateenvironment-records"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$93"}}],"$L94"]}]
95:T27bc,<p>Before it is evaluated, all ECMAScript code must be associated with a <dfn id="realm" variants="realms">realm</dfn>. Conceptually, a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref> consists of a set of intrinsic objects, an ECMAScript global environment, all of the ECMAScript code that is loaded within the scope of that global environment, and other associated state and resources.</p>
    <p>A <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref> is represented in this specification as a <dfn id="realm-record" variants="Realm Records">Realm Record</dfn> with the fields specified in <emu-xref href="#table-realm-record-fields"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#table-realm-record-fields">Table 19</a></emu-xref>:</p>

Each <var class="field">[[Site]]</var> value is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref> that is a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-TemplateLiteral">TemplateLiteral</a></emu-nt>. The associated <var class="field">[[Array]]</var> value is the corresponding template object that is passed to a tag function.</p>
            <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">Once a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref> becomes unreachable, the corresponding <var class="field">[[Array]]</var> is also unreachable, and it would be unobservable if an implementation removed the pair from the <var class="field">[[TemplateMap]]</var> list.</div></emu-note>

The list does not contain two different <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Records</a></emu-xref> <var>r1</var> and <var>r2</var> such that <emu-xref aoid="ModuleRequestsEqual"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-ModuleRequestsEqual">ModuleRequestsEqual</a></emu-xref>(<var>r1</var>, <var>r2</var>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</p>
            <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
              As mentioned in <emu-xref aoid="HostLoadImportedModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-HostLoadImportedModule">HostLoadImportedModule</a></emu-xref> (<emu-xref href="#note-HostLoadImportedModule-referrer-Realm-Record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#note-HostLoadImportedModule-referrer-Realm-Record">Note 1</a></emu-xref>), <var class="field">[[LoadedModules]]</var> in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record">Realm Records</a></emu-xref> is only used when running an <code>import()</code> expression in a context where there is no <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job-activescriptormodule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job-activescriptormodule">active script or module</a></emu-xref>.
