    7c:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-code-realms","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"93-realms","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"9.3"}]," Realms"],["$","$L77",null,{"id":"93-realms"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$95"}}],"$L96","$L97","$L98"]}]
99:T3848,<p>An <dfn variants="execution contexts">execution context</dfn> is a specification device that is used to track the runtime evaluation of code by an ECMAScript implementation. At any point in time, there is at most one execution context per <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> that is actually executing code. This is known as the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>'s <dfn id="running-execution-context" variants="running execution contexts">running execution context</dfn>. All references to the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> in this specification denote the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#surrounding-agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#surrounding-agent">surrounding agent</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <p>The <dfn id="execution-context-stack" variants="execution context stacks">execution context stack</dfn> is used to track execution contexts. The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> is always the top element of this stack. A new execution context is created whenever control is transferred from the executable code associated with the currently <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> to executable code that is not associated with that execution context. The newly created execution context is pushed onto the stack and becomes the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <p>An execution context contains whatever implementation specific state is necessary to track the execution progress of its associated code. Each execution context has at least the state components listed in <emu-xref href="#table-state-components-for-all-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#table-state-components-for-all-execution-contexts">Table 20</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <emu-table id="table-state-components-for-all-execution-contexts" caption="State Components for All Execution Contexts" oldids="table-22" data-num="20">
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>
              Component
            </th>
            <th>
              Purpose
            </th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tr>
          <td>
            code evaluation state
          </td>
          <td>
            Any state needed to perform, suspend, and resume evaluation of the code associated with this execution context.
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td>
            Function
          </td>
          <td>
            If this execution context is evaluating the code of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref>, then the value of this component is that <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref>. If the context is evaluating the code of a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt> or <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt>, the value is <emu-val>null</emu-val>.
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td>
            <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">Realm</a></emu-xref>
          </td>
          <td>
            The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record">Realm Record</a></emu-xref> from which associated code accesses ECMAScript resources.
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td>
            ScriptOrModule
          </td>
          <td>
            The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref> or <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record">Script Record</a></emu-xref> from which associated code originates. If there is no originating script or module, as is the case for the original execution context created in <emu-xref aoid="InitializeHostDefinedRealm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-initializehostdefinedrealm">InitializeHostDefinedRealm</a></emu-xref>, the value is <emu-val>null</emu-val>.
          </td>
        </tr>
      </table>
    </emu-table>
    <p><emu-xref aoid="Evaluation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-evaluation">Evaluation</a></emu-xref> of code by the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> may be suspended at various points defined within this specification. Once the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> has been suspended a different execution context may become the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> and commence evaluating its code. At some later time a suspended execution context may again become the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> and continue evaluating its code at the point where it had previously been suspended. Transition of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> status among execution contexts usually occurs in stack-like last-in/first-out manner. However, some ECMAScript features require non-LIFO transitions of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <p>The value of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">Realm</a></emu-xref> component of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> is also called <dfn id="current-realm">the current Realm Record</dfn>. The value of the Function component of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> is also called the <dfn id="active-function-object">active function object</dfn>.</p>
    <p><dfn id="ecmascript-code-execution-context" variants="ECMAScript code execution context">ECMAScript code execution contexts</dfn> have the additional state components listed in <emu-xref href="#table-additional-state-components-for-ecmascript-code-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#table-additional-state-components-for-ecmascript-code-execution-contexts">Table 21</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <emu-table id="table-additional-state-components-for-ecmascript-code-execution-contexts" caption="Additional State Components for ECMAScript Code Execution Contexts" oldids="table-23" data-num="21">
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>
              Component
            </th>
            <th>
              Purpose
            </th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tr>
          <td>
            LexicalEnvironment
          </td>
          <td>
            Identifies the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> used to resolve identifier references made by code within this execution context.
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td>
            VariableEnvironment
          </td>
          <td>
            Identifies the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> that holds bindings created by <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-VariableStatement">VariableStatement</a></emu-nt>s within this execution context.
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td>
            PrivateEnvironment
          </td>
          <td>
            Identifies the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#privateenvironment-record">PrivateEnvironment Record</a></emu-xref> that holds <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names">Private Names</a></emu-xref> created by <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassElement">ClassElement</a></emu-nt>s in the nearest containing class. <emu-val>null</emu-val> if there is no containing class.
          </td>
        </tr>
      </table>
    </emu-table>
    <p>The LexicalEnvironment and VariableEnvironment components of an execution context are always <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Records</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <p>Execution contexts representing the evaluation of Generators have the additional state components listed in <emu-xref href="#table-additional-state-components-for-generator-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#table-additional-state-components-for-generator-execution-contexts">Table 22</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <emu-table id="table-additional-state-components-for-generator-execution-contexts" caption="Additional State Components for Generator Execution Contexts" oldids="table-24" data-num="22">
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>
              Component
            </th>
            <th>
              Purpose
            </th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tr>
          <td>
            Generator
          </td>
          <td>
            The Generator that this execution context is evaluating.
          </td>
        </tr>
      </table>
    </emu-table>
    <p>In most situations only the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> (the top of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack">execution context stack</a></emu-xref>) is directly manipulated by algorithms within this specification. Hence when the terms “LexicalEnvironment”, and “VariableEnvironment” are used without qualification they are in reference to those components of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <p>An execution context is purely a specification mechanism and need not correspond to any particular artefact of an ECMAScript implementation. It is impossible for ECMAScript code to directly access or observe an execution context.</p>

    7d:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-execution-contexts","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"94-execution-contexts","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"9.4"}]," Execution Contexts"],["$","$L77",null,{"id":"94-execution-contexts"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$99"}}],"$L9a","$L9b","$L9c","$L9d","$L9e","$L9f","$La0"]}]
a1:T3c4b,<p>A <dfn id="job" variants="Jobs">Job</dfn> is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure">Abstract Closure</a></emu-xref> with no parameters that initiates an ECMAScript computation when no other ECMAScript computation is currently in progress.</p>
    <p><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Jobs</a></emu-xref> are scheduled for execution by ECMAScript <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">host environments</a></emu-xref> in a particular <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>. This specification describes the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-hook"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-hook">host hooks</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref aoid="HostEnqueueGenericJob"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-hostenqueuegenericjob">HostEnqueueGenericJob</a></emu-xref>, <emu-xref aoid="HostEnqueueFinalizationRegistryCleanupJob"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-host-cleanup-finalization-registry">HostEnqueueFinalizationRegistryCleanupJob</a></emu-xref>, <emu-xref aoid="HostEnqueuePromiseJob"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-hostenqueuepromisejob">HostEnqueuePromiseJob</a></emu-xref>, and <emu-xref aoid="HostEnqueueTimeoutJob"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-hostenqueuetimeoutjob">HostEnqueueTimeoutJob</a></emu-xref> to schedule jobs. The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-hook"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-hook">host hooks</a></emu-xref> in this specification are organized by the additional constraints imposed on the scheduling of jobs. <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">Hosts</a></emu-xref> may define additional <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-abstract-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-abstract-operations">abstract operations</a></emu-xref> which schedule jobs. Such operations accept a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Job</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure">Abstract Closure</a></emu-xref> and a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record">Realm Record</a></emu-xref> or <emu-val>null</emu-val>) as parameters. If a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record">Realm Record</a></emu-xref> is provided, these operations schedule the job to be performed at some future time in the provided <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref>, in the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> that owns the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref>. If <emu-val>null</emu-val> is provided instead for the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref>, then the job does not evaluate ECMAScript code. Their implementations must conform to the following requirements:</p>

    <ul>
      <li>At some future point in time, when there is no running context in the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> for which the job is scheduled and that <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>'s <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack">execution context stack</a></emu-xref> is empty, the implementation must:
        <ol>
          <li>Perform any <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined">host-defined</a></emu-xref> preparation steps.</li>
          <li><emu-not-ref>Invoke</emu-not-ref> the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Job</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure">Abstract Closure</a></emu-xref>.</li>
          <li>Perform any <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined">host-defined</a></emu-xref> cleanup steps, after which the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack">execution context stack</a></emu-xref> must be empty.</li>
        </ol>
      </li>
      <li>Only one <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Job</a></emu-xref> may be actively undergoing evaluation at any point in time in an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>.</li>
      <li>Once evaluation of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Job</a></emu-xref> starts, it must run to completion before evaluation of any other <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Job</a></emu-xref> starts in an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>.</li>
      <li>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure">Abstract Closure</a></emu-xref> must return a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion</a></emu-xref>, implementing its own handling of errors.</li>
    </ul>

    <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
      <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">Host environments</a></emu-xref> are not required to treat <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Jobs</a></emu-xref> uniformly with respect to scheduling. For example, web browsers and Node.js treat Promise-handling <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Jobs</a></emu-xref> as a higher priority than other work; future features may add <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Jobs</a></emu-xref> that are not treated at such a high priority.
    </div></emu-note>

    <p>At any particular time, <var>scriptOrModule</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record">Script Record</a></emu-xref>, a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref>, or <emu-val>null</emu-val>) is the <dfn id="job-activescriptormodule">active script or module</dfn> if all of the following conditions are true:</p>
    <ul>
      <li><emu-xref aoid="GetActiveScriptOrModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-getactivescriptormodule">GetActiveScriptOrModule</a></emu-xref>() is <var>scriptOrModule</var>.</li>
      <li>If <var>scriptOrModule</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#script-record">Script Record</a></emu-xref> or <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-abstract-module-records">Module Record</a></emu-xref>, let <var>executionContext</var> be the topmost <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref> on the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack">execution context stack</a></emu-xref> whose ScriptOrModule component is <var>scriptOrModule</var>. The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">Realm</a></emu-xref> component of <var>executionContext</var> is <var>scriptOrModule</var>.<var class="field">[[Realm]]</var>.</li>
    </ul>

    <p>At any particular time, an execution is <dfn id="job-preparedtoevaluatecode">prepared to evaluate ECMAScript code</dfn> if all of the following conditions are true:</p>
    <ul>
      <li>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack">execution context stack</a></emu-xref> is not empty.</li>
      <li>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">Realm</a></emu-xref> component of the topmost <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref> on the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack">execution context stack</a></emu-xref> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record">Realm Record</a></emu-xref>.</li>
    </ul>

    <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">Host environments</a></emu-xref> may prepare an execution to evaluate code by pushing <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution contexts</a></emu-xref> onto the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack">execution context stack</a></emu-xref>. The specific steps are <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#implementation-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#implementation-defined">implementation-defined</a></emu-xref>.</p>
      <p>The specific choice of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">Realm</a></emu-xref> is up to the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">host environment</a></emu-xref>. This initial <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref> and <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">Realm</a></emu-xref> is only in use before any callback function is invoked. When a callback function related to a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Job</a></emu-xref>, like a Promise handler, is invoked, the invocation pushes its own <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref> and <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">Realm</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

    <p>Particular kinds of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Jobs</a></emu-xref> have additional conformance requirements.</p>

    7e:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-jobs","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"95-jobs-and-host-operations-to-enqueue-jobs","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"9.5"}]," Jobs and Host Operations to Enqueue Jobs"],["$","$L77",null,{"id":"95-jobs-and-host-operations-to-enqueue-jobs"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$a1"}}],"$La2","$La3","$La4","$La5","$La6","$La7"]}]
a8:T39ea,<p>An <dfn id="agent" variants="agents">agent</dfn> comprises a set of ECMAScript <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution contexts</a></emu-xref>, an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack">execution context stack</a></emu-xref>, a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref>, an <dfn id="agent-record" variants="Agent Records">Agent Record</dfn>, and an <dfn id="executing-thread" variants="executing threads">executing thread</dfn>. Except for the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread">executing thread</a></emu-xref>, the constituents of an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> belong exclusively to that <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <p>An <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>'s <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread">executing thread</a></emu-xref> executes algorithmic steps on the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>'s <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution contexts</a></emu-xref> independently of other <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref>, except that an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread">executing thread</a></emu-xref> may be used as the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread">executing thread</a></emu-xref> by multiple <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref>, provided none of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> sharing the thread have an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record">Agent Record</a></emu-xref> whose <var class="field">[[CanBlock]]</var> field is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</p>
    <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>Some web browsers share a single <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread">executing thread</a></emu-xref> across multiple unrelated tabs of a browser window, for example.</p>
    </div></emu-note>
    <p>While an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>'s <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread">executing thread</a></emu-xref> is executing algorithmic steps, the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> is the <dfn id="surrounding-agent" variants="surrounding agents">surrounding agent</dfn> for those steps. The steps use the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#surrounding-agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#surrounding-agent">surrounding agent</a></emu-xref> to access the specification-level execution objects held within the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>: the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref>, the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#execution-context-stack">execution context stack</a></emu-xref>, and the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record">Agent Record</a></emu-xref>'s fields.</p>
    <p>An <dfn variants="agent signifiers">agent signifier</dfn> is a globally-unique opaque value used to identify an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">Agent</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <emu-table id="table-agent-record" caption="Agent Record Fields" data-num="24">
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Field Name</th>
            <th>Value</th>
            <th>Meaning</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tr>
          <td><var class="field">[[LittleEndian]]</var></td>
          <td>a Boolean</td>
          <td>The default value computed for the <em>isLittleEndian</em> parameter when it is needed by the algorithms <emu-xref aoid="GetValueFromBuffer"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/structured-data#sec-getvaluefrombuffer">GetValueFromBuffer</a></emu-xref> and <emu-xref aoid="SetValueInBuffer"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/structured-data#sec-setvalueinbuffer">SetValueInBuffer</a></emu-xref>. The choice is <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#implementation-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#implementation-defined">implementation-defined</a></emu-xref> and should be the alternative that is most efficient for the implementation.</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td><var class="field">[[CanBlock]]</var></td>
          <td>a Boolean</td>
          <td>Determines whether the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> can block or not.</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td><var class="field">[[Signifier]]</var></td>
          <td>an agent signifier</td>
          <td>Uniquely identifies the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> within its <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-agent-clusters"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-agent-clusters">agent cluster</a></emu-xref>.</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td><var class="field">[[IsLockFree1]]</var></td>
          <td>a Boolean</td>
          <td><emu-val>true</emu-val> if atomic operations on one-<emu-not-ref>byte values</emu-not-ref> are lock-free, <emu-val>false</emu-val> otherwise.</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td><var class="field">[[IsLockFree2]]</var></td>
          <td>a Boolean</td>
          <td><emu-val>true</emu-val> if atomic operations on two-<emu-not-ref>byte values</emu-not-ref> are lock-free, <emu-val>false</emu-val> otherwise.</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td><var class="field">[[IsLockFree8]]</var></td>
          <td>a Boolean</td>
          <td><emu-val>true</emu-val> if atomic operations on eight-<emu-not-ref>byte values</emu-not-ref> are lock-free, <emu-val>false</emu-val> otherwise.</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td><var class="field">[[CandidateExecution]]</var></td>
          <td>a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions">candidate execution</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Record</a></emu-xref></td>
          <td>See the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-memory-model"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-memory-model">memory model</a></emu-xref>.</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td><var class="field">[[KeptAlive]]</var></td>
          <td>a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of either Objects or Symbols</td>
          <td>Initially a new empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>, representing the list of objects and/or symbols to be kept alive until the end of the current <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Job</a></emu-xref></td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td><var class="field">[[ModuleAsyncEvaluationCount]]</var></td>
          <td>an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer">integer</a></emu-xref></td>
          <td>Initially 0, used to assign unique incrementing values to the <var class="field">[[AsyncEvaluationOrder]]</var> field of modules that are asynchronous or have asynchronous dependencies.</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td><var class="field">[[GlobalSymbolRegistry]]</var></td>
          <td>a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#sec-globalsymbolregistry-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#sec-globalsymbolregistry-records">GlobalSymbolRegistry Records</a></emu-xref></td>
          <td>Initially a new empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>, representing the list of Symbol values produced by <emu-xref href="#sec-symbol.for"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#sec-symbol.for">Symbol.for</a></emu-xref>.</td>
        </tr>
      </table>
    </emu-table>

    <p>The values of <var class="field">[[LittleEndian]]</var>, <var class="field">[[Signifier]]</var>, <var class="field">[[IsLockFree1]]</var>, <var class="field">[[IsLockFree2]]</var>, and <var class="field">[[IsLockFree8]]</var> cannot change.</p>

    <p>The <var class="field">[[GlobalSymbolRegistry]]</var> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> is append-only and never reassigned.</p>

    <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>The values of <var class="field">[[IsLockFree1]]</var>, <var class="field">[[IsLockFree2]]</var>, and <var class="field">[[IsLockFree8]]</var> are not necessarily determined by the hardware, but may also reflect implementation choices that can vary over time and between ECMAScript implementations.</p>

      <p>There is no <var class="field">[[IsLockFree4]]</var> field: 4-byte atomic operations are always lock-free.</p>

      <p>In practice, if an atomic operation is implemented with any type of lock the operation is not lock-free. Lock-free does not imply wait-free: there is no upper bound on how many machine steps may be required to complete a lock-free atomic operation.</p>

      <p>That an atomic access of size <em>n</em> is lock-free does not imply anything about the (perceived) atomicity of non-atomic accesses of size <em>n</em>, specifically, non-atomic accesses may still be performed as a sequence of several separate memory accesses. See <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-memory-model-fundamentals"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-memory-model-fundamentals">ReadSharedMemory</a></emu-xref> and <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-memory-model-fundamentals"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-memory-model-fundamentals">WriteSharedMemory</a></emu-xref> for details.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

    <emu-note data-num="3"><span class="note">Note 3</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>An <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> is a specification mechanism and need not correspond to any particular artefact of an ECMAScript implementation.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

    7f:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-agents","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"96-agents","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"9.6"}]," Agents"],["$","$L77",null,{"id":"96-agents"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$a8"}}],"$La9","$Laa","$Lab"]}]
ac:T3e55,<p>An <dfn variants="agent clusters">agent cluster</dfn> is a maximal set of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> that can communicate by operating on shared memory.</p>

    <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>Programs within different <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> may share memory by unspecified means. At a minimum, the backing memory for SharedArrayBuffers can be shared among the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> in the cluster.</p>

      <p>There may be <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> that can communicate by message passing that cannot share memory; they are never in the same agent cluster.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

    <p>Every <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> belongs to exactly one agent cluster.</p>

    <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> in a cluster need not all be alive at some particular point in time. If <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> <b>A</b> creates another <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> <b>B</b>, after which <b>A</b> terminates and <b>B</b> creates <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> <b>C</b>, the three <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> are in the same cluster if <b>A</b> could share some memory with <b>B</b> and <b>B</b> could share some memory with <b>C</b>.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

    <p>All <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> within a cluster must have the same value for the <var class="field">[[LittleEndian]]</var> field in their respective <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record">Agent Records</a></emu-xref>.</p>

    <emu-note data-num="3"><span class="note">Note 3</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>If different <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> within an agent cluster have different values of <var class="field">[[LittleEndian]]</var> it becomes hard to use shared memory for multi-byte data.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

    <p>All <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> within a cluster must have the same values for the <var class="field">[[IsLockFree1]]</var> field in their respective <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record">Agent Records</a></emu-xref>; similarly for the <var class="field">[[IsLockFree2]]</var> and <var class="field">[[IsLockFree8]]</var> fields.</p>

    <p>All <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> within a cluster must have different values for the <var class="field">[[Signifier]]</var> field in their respective <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record">Agent Records</a></emu-xref>.</p>

    <p>An embedding may deactivate (stop forward progress) or activate (resume forward progress) an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> without the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>'s knowledge or cooperation. If the embedding does so, it must not leave some <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> in the cluster active while other <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> in the cluster are deactivated indefinitely.</p>

    <emu-note data-num="4"><span class="note">Note 4</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>The purpose of the preceding restriction is to avoid a situation where an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> deadlocks or starves because another <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> has been deactivated. For example, if an HTML shared worker that has a lifetime independent of documents in any windows were allowed to share memory with the dedicated worker of such an independent document, and the document and its dedicated worker were to be deactivated while the dedicated worker holds a lock (say, the document is pushed into its window's history), and the shared worker then tries to acquire the lock, then the shared worker will be blocked until the dedicated worker is activated again, if ever. Meanwhile other workers trying to access the shared worker from other windows will starve.</p>

      <p>The implication of the restriction is that it will not be possible to share memory between <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> that don't belong to the same suspend/wake collective within the embedding.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

    <p>An embedding may terminate an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> without any of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>'s cluster's other <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref>' prior knowledge or cooperation. If an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> is terminated not by programmatic action of its own or of another <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> in the cluster but by forces external to the cluster, then the embedding must choose one of two strategies: Either terminate all the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> in the cluster, or provide reliable APIs that allow the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> in the cluster to coordinate so that at least one remaining member of the cluster will be able to detect the termination, with the termination data containing enough information to identify the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> that was terminated.</p>

    <emu-note data-num="5"><span class="note">Note 5</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>Examples of that type of termination are: operating systems or users terminating <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> that are running in separate processes; the embedding itself terminating an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> that is running in-process with the other <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> when per-<emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> resource accounting indicates that the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> is runaway.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

    <p>Each of the following specification values, and values transitively reachable from them, belong to exactly one agent cluster.</p>
    <ul>
      <li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions">candidate execution</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Record</a></emu-xref></li>
      <li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-data-blocks"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-data-blocks">Shared Data Block</a></emu-xref></li>
      <li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/structured-data#sec-waiterlist-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/structured-data#sec-waiterlist-records">WaiterList Record</a></emu-xref></li>
    </ul>

    <p>Prior to any evaluation of any ECMAScript code by any <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> in a cluster, the <var class="field">[[CandidateExecution]]</var> field of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record">Agent Record</a></emu-xref> for all <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> in the cluster is set to the initial <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions">candidate execution</a></emu-xref>. The initial <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions">candidate execution</a></emu-xref> is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions">empty candidate execution</a></emu-xref> whose <var class="field">[[EventsRecords]]</var> field is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> containing, for each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>, an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-agent-event-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-agent-event-records">Agent Events Record</a></emu-xref> whose <var class="field">[[AgentSignifier]]</var> field is that <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>'s <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-agents"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-agents">agent signifier</a></emu-xref>, and whose <var class="field">[[EventList]]</var> and <var class="field">[[AgentSynchronizesWith]]</var> fields are empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Lists</a></emu-xref>.</p>

    <emu-note data-num="6"><span class="note">Note 6</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>All <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> in an agent cluster share the same <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions">candidate execution</a></emu-xref> in its <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record">Agent Record</a></emu-xref>'s <var class="field">[[CandidateExecution]]</var> field. The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-candidate-executions">candidate execution</a></emu-xref> is a specification mechanism used by the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-memory-model"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-memory-model">memory model</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

    <emu-note data-num="7"><span class="note">Note 7</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>An agent cluster is a specification mechanism and need not correspond to any particular artefact of an ECMAScript implementation.</p>
    </div></emu-note>
  80:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-agent-clusters","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"97-agent-clusters","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"9.7"}]," Agent Clusters"],["$","$L77",null,{"id":"97-agent-clusters"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$ac"}}]]}]
ad:Tec2,<p>For an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> to <em>make forward progress</em> is for it to perform an evaluation step according to this specification.</p>
    <p>An <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> becomes <em>blocked</em> when its <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref> waits synchronously and indefinitely for an external event. Only <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> whose <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent-record">Agent Record</a></emu-xref>'s <var class="field">[[CanBlock]]</var> field is <emu-val>true</emu-val> can become blocked in this sense. An <em>unblocked</em> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> is one that is not blocked.</p>

    <p>Implementations must ensure that:</p>
    <ul>
      <li>every unblocked <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> with a dedicated <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread">executing thread</a></emu-xref> eventually makes forward progress</li>
      <li>in a set of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref> that share an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#executing-thread">executing thread</a></emu-xref>, one <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> eventually makes forward progress</li>
      <li>an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> does not cause another <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref> to become blocked except via explicit APIs that provide blocking.</li>
    </ul>

    <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>This, along with the liveness guarantee in the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-memory-model"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-memory-model">memory model</a></emu-xref>, ensures that all <emu-const>seq-cst</emu-const> writes eventually become observable to all <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agents</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    </div></emu-note>
  81:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-forward-progress","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"98-forward-progress","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"9.8"}]," Forward Progress"],["$","$L77",null,{"id":"98-forward-progress"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$ad"}}]]}]
ae:T114e,<p>This specification does not make any guarantees that any object or symbol will be garbage collected. Objects or symbols which are not <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-liveness"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-liveness">live</a></emu-xref> may be released after long periods of time, or never at all. For this reason, this specification uses the term “may” when describing behaviour triggered by garbage collection.</p>

      <p>The semantics of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRefs</a></emu-xref> and <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor">FinalizationRegistrys</a></emu-xref> is based on two operations which happen at particular points in time:</p>

      <ul>
        <li>
          When <code>WeakRef.prototype.deref</code> is called, the referent (if <emu-val>undefined</emu-val> is not returned) is kept alive so that subsequent, synchronous accesses also return the same value. This list is reset when synchronous work is done using the <emu-xref aoid="ClearKeptObjects"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-clear-kept-objects">ClearKeptObjects</a></emu-xref> abstract operation.
        </li>

        <li>
          When an object or symbol which is registered with a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor">FinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref> becomes unreachable, a call of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor">FinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref>'s cleanup callback may eventually be made, after synchronous ECMAScript execution completes. The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor">FinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref> cleanup is performed with the <emu-xref aoid="CleanupFinalizationRegistry"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-cleanup-finalization-registry">CleanupFinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref> abstract operation.
        </li>
      </ul>

      <p>Neither of these actions (<emu-xref aoid="ClearKeptObjects"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-clear-kept-objects">ClearKeptObjects</a></emu-xref> or <emu-xref aoid="CleanupFinalizationRegistry"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-cleanup-finalization-registry">CleanupFinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref>) may interrupt synchronous ECMAScript execution. Because <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">hosts</a></emu-xref> may assemble longer, synchronous ECMAScript execution runs, this specification defers the scheduling of <emu-xref aoid="ClearKeptObjects"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-clear-kept-objects">ClearKeptObjects</a></emu-xref> and <emu-xref aoid="CleanupFinalizationRegistry"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-cleanup-finalization-registry">CleanupFinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref> to the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">host environment</a></emu-xref>.</p>

      <p>Some ECMAScript implementations include garbage collector implementations which run in the background, including when ECMAScript is idle. Letting the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">host environment</a></emu-xref> schedule <emu-xref aoid="CleanupFinalizationRegistry"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-cleanup-finalization-registry">CleanupFinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref> allows it to resume ECMAScript execution in order to run finalizer work, which may free up held values, reducing overall memory usage.</p>

b5:Tc79,<p>The abstract operation CanBeHeldWeakly takes argument <var>arg</var> (an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types">ECMAScript language value</a></emu-xref>) and returns a Boolean. It returns <emu-val>true</emu-val> if and only if <var>arg</var> is suitable for use as a weak reference. Only values that are suitable for use as a weak reference may be a key of a WeakMap, an element of a WeakSet, the target of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRef</a></emu-xref>, or one of the targets of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor">FinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref>.     <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">If <var>arg</var> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">is an Object</a></emu-xref>, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">If <var>arg</var> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types-symbol-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types-symbol-type">is a Symbol</a></emu-xref> and <emu-xref aoid="KeyForSymbol"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#sec-keyforsymbol">KeyForSymbol</a></emu-xref>(<var>arg</var>) is <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">Return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
    <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>A language value without <emu-xref href="#sec-identity"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-identity">language identity</a></emu-xref> can be manifested without prior reference and is unsuitable for use as a weak reference. A Symbol value produced by <emu-xref href="#sec-symbol.for"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#sec-symbol.for">Symbol.for</a></emu-xref>, unlike other Symbol values, does not have language identity and is unsuitable for use as a weak reference. <emu-xref href="#sec-well-known-symbols"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-well-known-symbols">Well-known symbols</a></emu-xref> are likely to never be collected, but are nonetheless treated as suitable for use as a weak reference because they are limited in number and therefore manageable by a variety of implementation approaches. However, any value associated to a well-known symbol in a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-liveness"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-liveness">live</a></emu-xref> WeakMap is unlikely to be collected and could “leak” memory resources in implementations.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

b6:T731,<p>The CreateMutableBinding concrete method of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records">Declarative Environment Record</a></emu-xref> <var>envRecord</var> takes arguments <var>name</var> (a String) and <var>deletable</var> (a Boolean) and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> <emu-const>unused</emu-const>. c0:Teda,<p>Each <dfn variants="Object Environment Records">Object Environment Record</dfn> is associated with an object called its <em>binding object</em>. An Object Environment Record binds the set of string identifier names that directly correspond to the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-name"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-name">property names</a></emu-xref> of its binding object. <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">Property keys</a></emu-xref> that are not strings in the form of an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-IdentifierName">IdentifierName</a></emu-nt> are not included in the set of bound identifiers. Both own and inherited properties are included in the set regardless of the setting of their <var class="field">[[Enumerable]]</var> attribute. Because properties can be dynamically added and deleted from objects, the set of identifiers bound by an Object Environment Record may potentially change as a side-effect of any operation that adds or deletes properties. Any bindings that are created as a result of such a side-effect are considered to be a mutable binding even if the Writable attribute of the corresponding property is <emu-val>false</emu-val>. Immutable bindings do not exist for Object Environment Records.</p>
        <p>Object Environment Records created for <code>with</code> statements (<emu-xref href="#sec-with-statement"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#sec-with-statement">14.11</a></emu-xref>) can provide their binding object as an implicit <emu-val>this</emu-val> value for use in function calls. The capability is controlled by a Boolean <var class="field">[[IsWithEnvironment]]</var> field.</p>
        <p>Object Environment Records have the additional state fields listed in <emu-xref href="#table-additional-fields-of-object-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#table-additional-fields-of-object-environment-records">Table 15</a></emu-xref>.</p>

cc:T1730,<p>A <dfn variants="Function Environment Records">Function Environment Record</dfn> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records">Declarative Environment Record</a></emu-xref> that is used to represent the top-level scope of a function and, if the function is not an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFunction">ArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> function or <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncArrowFunction">AsyncArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> function, provides a <code>this</code> binding. If a function is not an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFunction">ArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> function or <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncArrowFunction">AsyncArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> function and references <code>super</code>, its Function Environment Record also contains the state that is used to perform <code>super</code> method invocations from within the function.</p>
