        <p>Function Environment Records support all of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records">Declarative Environment Record</a></emu-xref> methods listed in <emu-xref href="#table-abstract-methods-of-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#table-abstract-methods-of-environment-records">Table 14</a></emu-xref> and share the same specifications for all of those methods except for HasThisBinding, GetThisBinding, and HasSuperBinding.</p>

        8a:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-function-environment-records","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"9113-function-environment-records","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"9.1.1.3"}]," Function Environment Records"],["$","$L77",null,{"id":"9113-function-environment-records"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$cc"}}],"$Lcd","$Lce","$Lcf","$Ld0","$Ld1"]}]
d2:T29af,<p>A <dfn variants="Global Environment Records">Global Environment Record</dfn> is used to represent the outer most scope that is shared by all of the ECMAScript <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt> elements that are processed in a common <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref>. A Global Environment Record provides the bindings for built-in globals (clause <emu-xref href="#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">19</a></emu-xref>), properties of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">global object</a></emu-xref>, and for all top-level declarations (<emu-xref href="#sec-static-semantics-toplevellexicallyscopeddeclarations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-toplevellexicallyscopeddeclarations">8.2.9</a></emu-xref>, <emu-xref href="#sec-static-semantics-toplevelvarscopeddeclarations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-toplevelvarscopeddeclarations">8.2.11</a></emu-xref>) that occur within a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt>.</p>
        <p>A Global Environment Record is logically a single record but it is specified as a composite encapsulating an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-object-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-object-environment-records">Object Environment Record</a></emu-xref> and a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records">Declarative Environment Record</a></emu-xref>. The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-object-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-object-environment-records">Object Environment Record</a></emu-xref> has as its base object the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">global object</a></emu-xref> of the associated <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record">Realm Record</a></emu-xref>. This <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">global object</a></emu-xref> is the value returned by the Global Environment Record's GetThisBinding concrete method. The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-object-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-object-environment-records">Object Environment Record</a></emu-xref> component of a Global Environment Record contains the bindings for all built-in globals (clause <emu-xref href="#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">19</a></emu-xref>) and all bindings introduced by a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionDeclaration">FunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorDeclaration">GeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionDeclaration">AsyncFunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorDeclaration">AsyncGeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, or <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-VariableStatement">VariableStatement</a></emu-nt> contained in global code. The bindings for all other ECMAScript declarations in global code are contained in the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records">Declarative Environment Record</a></emu-xref> component of the Global Environment Record.</p>
        <p>Properties may be created directly on a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">global object</a></emu-xref>. Hence, the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-object-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-object-environment-records">Object Environment Record</a></emu-xref> component of a Global Environment Record may contain both bindings created explicitly by <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionDeclaration">FunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorDeclaration">GeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionDeclaration">AsyncFunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorDeclaration">AsyncGeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, or <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-VariableDeclaration">VariableDeclaration</a></emu-nt> declarations and bindings created implicitly as properties of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">global object</a></emu-xref>.</p>
        <p>Global Environment Records have the additional fields listed in <emu-xref href="#table-additional-fields-of-global-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#table-additional-fields-of-global-environment-records">Table 17</a></emu-xref>.</p>

              </td>
              <td>
                Binding object is the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/global-object#sec-global-object">global object</a></emu-xref>. It contains global built-in bindings as well as <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionDeclaration">FunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorDeclaration">GeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionDeclaration">AsyncFunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorDeclaration">AsyncGeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, and <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-VariableDeclaration">VariableDeclaration</a></emu-nt> bindings in global code for the associated <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref>.

              <td>
                <var class="field">[[DeclarativeRecord]]</var>
              </td>
              <td>
                a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records">Declarative Environment Record</a></emu-xref>
              </td>
              <td>
                <emu-not-ref>Contains</emu-not-ref> bindings for all declarations in global code for the associated <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref> code except for <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionDeclaration">FunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorDeclaration">GeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionDeclaration">AsyncFunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorDeclaration">AsyncGeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, and <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-VariableDeclaration">VariableDeclaration</a></emu-nt> bindings.
              </td>

e4:T677,<p>A <dfn variants="Module Environment Records">Module Environment Record</dfn> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records">Declarative Environment Record</a></emu-xref> that is used to represent the outer scope of an ECMAScript <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt>. In additional to normal mutable and immutable bindings, Module Environment Records also provide immutable import bindings which are bindings that provide indirect access to a target binding that exists in another <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref>.</p>
        <p>Module Environment Records support all of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-declarative-environment-records">Declarative Environment Record</a></emu-xref> methods listed in <emu-xref href="#table-abstract-methods-of-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#table-abstract-methods-of-environment-records">Table 14</a></emu-xref> and share the same specifications for all of those methods except for GetBindingValue, DeleteBinding, HasThisBinding, and GetThisBinding.</p>

        e5:Tcf5,<p>The GetBindingValue concrete method of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-module-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-module-environment-records">Module Environment Record</a></emu-xref> <var>envRecord</var> takes arguments <var>name</var> (a String) and <var>strict</var> (a Boolean) and returns either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types">ECMAScript language value</a></emu-xref> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>. It returns the value of its bound identifier whose name is <var>name</var>. However, if the binding is an indirect binding the value of the target binding is returned. If the binding exists but is uninitialized a <emu-val>ReferenceError</emu-val> is thrown. It performs the following steps when called:</p>
          <emu-alg><ol><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: <var>strict</var> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: <var>envRecord</var> has a binding for <var>name</var>.</li><li>If the binding for <var>name</var> is an indirect binding, then<ol><li>Let <var>module</var> and <var>targetName</var> be the indirection values provided when this binding for <var>name</var> was created.</li><li>Let <var>targetEnv</var> be <var>module</var>.<var class="field">[[Environment]]</var>.</li><li class="exit">If <var>targetEnv</var> is <emu-const>empty</emu-const>, throw a <emu-val>ReferenceError</emu-val> exception.</li><li class="exit">Return ? <emu-meta effects="user-code"><var>targetEnv</var>.GetBindingValue(<var>targetName</var>, <emu-val>true</emu-val>)</emu-meta>.</li></ol></li><li class="exit">If the binding for <var>name</var> in <var>envRecord</var> is an uninitialized binding, throw a <emu-val>ReferenceError</emu-val> exception.</li><li class="exit">Return the value currently bound to <var>name</var> in <var>envRecord</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
          <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
            <p><var>strict</var> will always be <emu-val>true</emu-val> because a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt> is always <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-strict-mode-code"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-strict-mode-code">strict mode code</a></emu-xref>.</p>
          </div></emu-note>

fa:T1192,<p>A <dfn variants="JobCallback Records">JobCallback Record</dfn> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Record</a></emu-xref> value used to store a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> and a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined">host-defined</a></emu-xref> value. <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">Function objects</a></emu-xref> that are invoked via a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Job</a></emu-xref> enqueued by the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host">host</a></emu-xref> may have additional <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined">host-defined</a></emu-xref> context. To propagate the state, <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Job</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure">Abstract Closures</a></emu-xref> should not capture and call <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function objects</a></emu-xref> directly. Instead, use <emu-xref aoid="HostMakeJobCallback"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-hostmakejobcallback">HostMakeJobCallback</a></emu-xref> and <emu-xref aoid="HostCallJobCallback"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-hostcalljobcallback">HostCallJobCallback</a></emu-xref>.</p>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The WHATWG HTML specification (<a href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/">https://html.spec.whatwg.org/</a>), for example, uses the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined">host-defined</a></emu-xref> value to propagate the incumbent settings object for Promise callbacks.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

fe:T1842,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-defined">host-defined</a></emu-xref> abstract operation HostEnqueuePromiseJob takes arguments <var>job</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Job</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure">Abstract Closure</a></emu-xref>) and <var>realm</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record">Realm Record</a></emu-xref> or <emu-val>null</emu-val>) and returns <emu-const>unused</emu-const>. It schedules <var>job</var> to be performed at some future time. The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-abstract-closure">Abstract Closures</a></emu-xref> used with this algorithm are intended to be related to the handling of Promises, or otherwise, to be scheduled with equal priority to Promise handling operations.</p>

      <p>An implementation of HostEnqueuePromiseJob must conform to the requirements in <emu-xref href="#sec-jobs"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-jobs">9.5</a></emu-xref> as well as the following:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>If <var>realm</var> is not <emu-val>null</emu-val>, each time <var>job</var> is invoked the implementation must perform <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#implementation-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#implementation-defined">implementation-defined</a></emu-xref> steps such that execution is <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job-preparedtoevaluatecode"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job-preparedtoevaluatecode">prepared to evaluate ECMAScript code</a></emu-xref> at the time of <var>job</var>'s invocation.</li>
        <li>Let <var>scriptOrModule</var> be <emu-xref aoid="GetActiveScriptOrModule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-getactivescriptormodule">GetActiveScriptOrModule</a></emu-xref>() at the time HostEnqueuePromiseJob is invoked. If <var>realm</var> is not <emu-val>null</emu-val>, each time <var>job</var> is invoked the implementation must perform <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#implementation-defined"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#implementation-defined">implementation-defined</a></emu-xref> steps such that <var>scriptOrModule</var> is the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job-activescriptormodule"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job-activescriptormodule">active script or module</a></emu-xref> at the time of <var>job</var>'s invocation.</li>
        <li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Jobs</a></emu-xref> must run in the same order as the HostEnqueuePromiseJob invocations that scheduled them.</li>
      </ul>

      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The <var>realm</var> for <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Jobs</a></emu-xref> returned by <emu-xref aoid="NewPromiseResolveThenableJob"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-newpromiseresolvethenablejob">NewPromiseResolveThenableJob</a></emu-xref> is usually the result of calling <emu-xref aoid="GetFunctionRealm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-getfunctionrealm">GetFunctionRealm</a></emu-xref> on the <var>then</var> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref>. The <var>realm</var> for <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Jobs</a></emu-xref> returned by <emu-xref aoid="NewPromiseReactionJob"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-newpromisereactionjob">NewPromiseReactionJob</a></emu-xref> is usually the result of calling <emu-xref aoid="GetFunctionRealm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-getfunctionrealm">GetFunctionRealm</a></emu-xref> on the handler if the handler is not <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>. If the handler is <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>, <var>realm</var> is <emu-val>null</emu-val>. For both kinds of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#job">Jobs</a></emu-xref>, when <emu-xref aoid="GetFunctionRealm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-getfunctionrealm">GetFunctionRealm</a></emu-xref> completes abnormally (i.e. called on a revoked Proxy), <var>realm</var> is <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#current-realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#current-realm">the current Realm Record</a></emu-xref> at the time of the <emu-xref aoid="GetFunctionRealm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-getfunctionrealm">GetFunctionRealm</a></emu-xref> call. When the <var>realm</var> is <emu-val>null</emu-val>, no user ECMAScript code will be evaluated and no new ECMAScript objects (e.g. Error objects) will be created. The WHATWG HTML specification (<a href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/">https://html.spec.whatwg.org/</a>), for example, uses <var>realm</var> to check for the ability to run script and for the <a href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/#entry">entry</a> concept.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

    ab:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-IncrementModuleAsyncEvaluationCount","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"963-incrementmoduleasyncevaluationcount--","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"9.6.3"}]," IncrementModuleAsyncEvaluationCount ( )"],["$","$L77",null,{"id":"963-incrementmoduleasyncevaluationcount--"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$101"}}]]}]
102:T164d,<p>For some set of objects and/or symbols <var>objSet</var> a <dfn>hypothetical WeakRef-oblivious</dfn> execution with respect to <var>objSet</var> is an execution whereby the abstract operation <emu-xref aoid="WeakRefDeref"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weakrefderef">WeakRefDeref</a></emu-xref> of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRef</a></emu-xref> whose referent is an element of <var>objSet</var> always returns <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>.</p>

      <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
        <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRef</a></emu-xref>-obliviousness, together with liveness, capture two notions. One, that a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRef</a></emu-xref> itself does not keep its referent alive. Two, that cycles in liveness does not imply that a value is live. To be concrete, if determining <var>v</var>'s liveness depends on determining the liveness of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRef</a></emu-xref> referent, <var>r</var>, <var>r</var>'s liveness cannot assume <var>v</var>'s liveness, which would be circular reasoning.
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
        <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRef</a></emu-xref>-obliviousness is defined on sets of objects or symbols instead of individual values to account for cycles. If it were defined on individual values, then a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRef</a></emu-xref> referent in a cycle will be considered live even though its identity is only observed via other <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRef</a></emu-xref> referents in the cycle.
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="3"><span class="note">Note 3</span><div class="note-contents">
        Colloquially, we say that an individual object or symbol is live if every set containing it is live.
      </div></emu-note>

      <p>At any point during evaluation, a set of objects and/or symbols <var>objSet</var> is considered <dfn>live</dfn> if either of the following conditions is met:</p>

      <ul>
        <li>
          Any element in <var>objSet</var> is included in any <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#agent">agent</a></emu-xref>'s <var class="field">[[KeptAlive]]</var> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>.
        </li>
        <li>
          There exists a valid future hypothetical WeakRef-oblivious execution with respect to <var>objSet</var> that observes the identity of any value in <var>objSet</var>.
        </li>
      </ul>
      <emu-note data-num="4"><span class="note">Note 4</span><div class="note-contents">
        The second condition above intends to capture the intuition that a value is live if its identity is observable via non-<emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRef</a></emu-xref> means. A value's identity may be observed by observing a strict equality comparison or observing the value being used as key in a Map.
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="5"><span class="note">Note 5</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Presence of an object or a symbol in a field, an internal slot, or a property does not imply that the value is live. For example if the value in question is never passed back to the program, then it cannot be observed.</p>

        <p>This is the case for keys in a WeakMap, members of a WeakSet, as well as the <var class="field">[[WeakRefTarget]]</var> and <var class="field">[[UnregisterToken]]</var> fields of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor">FinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref> Cell record.</p>

        <p>The above definition implies that, if a key in a WeakMap is not live, then its corresponding value is not necessarily live either.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="6"><span class="note">Note 6</span><div class="note-contents">
        Liveness is the lower bound for guaranteeing which <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRefs</a></emu-xref> engines must not empty. Liveness as defined here is undecidable. In practice, engines use conservative approximations such as reachability. There is expected to be significant implementation leeway.
      </div></emu-note>

103:T1f83,<p>At any time, if a set of objects and/or symbols <var>objSet</var> is not <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-liveness"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-liveness">live</a></emu-xref>, an ECMAScript implementation may perform the following steps atomically:</p>

      <emu-alg><ol><li>For each element <var>value</var> of <var>objSet</var>, do<ol><li>For each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a <var>fg</var> such that <var>fg</var>.<var class="field">[[Cells]]</var> contains a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a       <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Together with the definition of liveness, this clause prescribes optimizations that an implementation may apply regarding <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRefs</a></emu-xref>.</p>

        <p>It is possible to access an object without observing its identity. Optimizations such as dead variable elimination and scalar replacement on properties of non-escaping objects whose identity is not observed are allowed. These optimizations are thus allowed to observably empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRefs</a></emu-xref> that point to such objects.</p>

        <p>On the other hand, if an object's identity is observable, and that object is in the <var class="field">[[WeakRefTarget]]</var> internal slot of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRef</a></emu-xref>, optimizations such as rematerialization that observably empty the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRef</a></emu-xref> are prohibited.</p>

        <p>Because calling <emu-xref aoid="HostEnqueueFinalizationRegistryCleanupJob"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-host-cleanup-finalization-registry">HostEnqueueFinalizationRegistryCleanupJob</a></emu-xref> is optional, registered objects in a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor">FinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref> do not necessarily hold that <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor">FinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-liveness"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-liveness">live</a></emu-xref>. Implementations may omit <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor">FinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref> callbacks for any reason, e.g., if the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-finalization-registry-constructor">FinalizationRegistry</a></emu-xref> itself becomes dead, or if the application is shutting down.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Implementations are not obligated to empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRefs</a></emu-xref> for maximal sets of non-<emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-liveness"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-liveness">live</a></emu-xref> objects or symbols.</p>
        <p>If an implementation chooses a non-<emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-liveness"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-liveness">live</a></emu-xref> set <var>objSet</var> in which to empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRefs</a></emu-xref>, this definition requires that it empties <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRefs</a></emu-xref> for all values in <var>objSet</var> simultaneously. In other words, it is not conformant for an implementation to empty a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRef</a></emu-xref> pointing to a value <var>v</var> without emptying out other <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/managing-memory#sec-weak-ref-constructor">WeakRefs</a></emu-xref> that, if not emptied, could result in an execution that observes the value of <var>v</var>.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

          <emu-alg><ol><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: <var>envRecord</var> does not already have a binding for <var>name</var>.</li><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: When <var>targetModule</var>.<var class="field">[[Environment]]</var> is instantiated, it will have a direct binding for <var>targetName</var>.</li><li>Create an immutable indirect binding in <var>envRecord</var> for <var>name</var> that references <var>targetModule</var> and <var>targetName</var> as its target binding and record that the binding is initialized.</li><li class="exit">Return <emu-const>unused</emu-const>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
